word-wrap: break-word;'>I</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>he/ she</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>we</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>they</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>you</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>I/ we</td></tr><tr><td rowspan="13">Tân ngữ</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>me</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>him/ her</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>us</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>them</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>you</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>me/ us</td></tr><tr><td rowspan="4" colspan="2">Đại từ phản thân (Reflexive pronouns) ourselves yourself yourselves</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>myself</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>himself/ herself</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>themselves</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>myself</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>ourselves</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td></tr><tr><td rowspan="3" colspan="2">Đại từ sở hữu (Possessive pronouns) ours yours</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>mine</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>his/ hers</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>theirs</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>mine/ ours</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td></tr><tr><td rowspan="3" colspan="2">Tính từ sở hữu (Possessive adjectives) our your</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>my</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>his/ her</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>their</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>my/ our</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td></tr></table>

 Ex: "Your ring looks so unique," Hilda said.

→ Hilda told me (that) my ring looked so unique.

"This grey bag is mine," he said. → He said that grey bag was his.

b. Bảng chuyển đổi từ chỉ định, trạng từ, hoặc cụm trạng từ chỉ nơi chốn, thời gian



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Direct Speech</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Indirect Speech</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>this</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>that/ the</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>this/ that (là đại từ )</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>it</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>these</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>those</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>here</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>there</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>now</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>then/ at that time/ at that moment</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>today</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>that day</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>tonight</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>that night</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>yesterday</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>the day before/ the previous day</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>the day before yesterday</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>two days before</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>last week</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>the week before/ the previous week</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>this morning</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>that morning</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>ago</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>before</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>a month ago</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>a month before</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>tomorrow</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>the next day/ the day after/ the following day</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>next year</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>the following year/ the next year/ the year after</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>in one hour's time</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>one hour later</td></tr></table>





☑ "My family arrived here a long time ago," Sue said.

→ Sue said (that) her family had arrived there a long time before.

"They make the best sushi here," said Makoto.

→ Makoto told me (that) they made the best sushi there.

c. Bảng chuyển đổi thì



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Direct Speech</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Indirect Speech</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Present Simple</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Past Simple</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>He said, “I work in a bank.”</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>He said (that) he worked in a bank.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Present Continuous</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Past Continuous</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Hannah said, “I am reading a book.”</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Hannah told me (that) she was reading a book.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Present Perfect</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Past Perfect</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>They said, “We have finished the project.”</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>They said (that) they had finished the project.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Present Perfect Continuous</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Past Perfect Continuous</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Jo said, “I have been waiting for an hour.”</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Jo said (that) he had been waiting for an hour.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Past Simple</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Past Perfect</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>She said, “I visited Paris last year.”</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>She told me (that) she had visited Paris the year before.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Past Continuous</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Past Perfect Continuous</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Albert said, “I was cooking at 5 o’clock yesterday.”</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Albert said (that) he had been cooking at 5 o’clock the previous day.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Past Perfect*</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Past Perfect*</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Trisha said, “I had already cleaned my room before you came.”</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Trisha said to me (that) she had already cleaned her room before I came.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Past Perfect Continuous*</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Past Perfect Continuous*</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Nicholas said, "I had been working out for two hours."</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Nicholas said (that) he had been working out for two hours.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Future Simple She said, "I will call you tomorrow."</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Future in the past She said (that) he would call me the next day.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Future Continuous Amber said, "We'll be going out at 8 P.M."</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Future Continuous in the past Amber told me (that) they would be going out at 8 P.M.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Future Perfect Mr Clark said, "I will have completed this building project by the end of next month."</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Future Perfect in the past Mr. Clark said that he would have completed that building project by the end of the following month.</td></tr><tr><td colspan="2">Modal verbs - Modal verbs in the past</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Can Usagi said, "I can speak Italian."</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Could Usagi said (that) she could speak Italian.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Must They said, "We must stay focused on our goals."</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Must/ had to They said (that) they must/ had to stay focused on their goals.</td></tr></table>





*: Không lùi thì

### d. Trường hợp đặc biệt với động từ tình thái Shall và May



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Các trường hợp</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Ví dụ</td></tr><tr><td rowspan="3">Shall</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Khi shall dùng cho tương lai đơn (Future Simple), ta đổi shall → would</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>“I shall attend the meeting,” Carol said. → Carol said (that) she would attend the meeting.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Khi shall dùng để đề nghị hoặc hỏi ý kiến, ta đổi shall → should</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>“Shall we go to the cinema?” he asked. → He asked if we should go to the cinema.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Khi shall dùng để diễn tả mệnh lệnh, quy định hoặc cam kết mạnh mẽ, ta đổi shall → should</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>“You shall not pass!” the guard said. → The guard said (that) we should not pass.</td></tr><tr><td rowspan="2">May</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Khi may mang ý nghĩa về một khả năng xảy ra (possibility), ta đổi may → might</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>He said, “It may rain later.” → He told me (that) it might rain later.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Khi may mang nghĩa cho phép, ta đổi may → could</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>My mom said, “You may watch TV after finishing your homework.” → My mom told me (that) I could watch TV after finishing my homework.</td></tr></table>

### e. Trường hợp không thay đổi thì của động từ trong lời nói gián tiếp

<div style="text-align: center;"><div style="text-align: center;">Thông thường, khi chuyển từ lời nói trực tiếp sang lời nói gián tiếp, thì của động từ thường lùi về một thì trong quá khứ. Tuy nhiên, có một số trường hợp đặc biệt mà thì của động từ được giữ nguyên (hay lùi thì) vẫn đều được.</div> </div>




<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Trường hợp</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Lùi thi</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Giữ nguyên</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Ví dụ</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Khi trình bày sự thật khoa học, chân lý, thói quen không bao giờ thay đổi.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>✓</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>✓ Cách này phổ biến hơn.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>The teacher said, “Water boils at 100 degrees Celsius.” → The teacher said (that) water boils/boiled at 100 degrees Celsius.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Khi động từ tường thuật (say, tell...) ở thì hiện tại đơn, hiện tại tiếp diễn, hiện tại hoàn thành hoặc tương lai đơn.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>✓</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>He says, “I met Felicia last night.” → He says (that) he met Felicia last night. Loan has told us, “The meeting will be on Friday.” → Loan has told us (that) the meeting will be on Friday.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Khi lời nói trực tiếp là câu điều kiện loại 2.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>✓</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>✓</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>She said, “If I knew the answer, I would tell you.” → She said (that) if she knew the answer, she would tell me.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>* Lưu ý: Trạng từ chỉ thời gian/nơi chốn vẫn thay đổi.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Cách này phổ biến hơn.</td><td colspan="2">[Or: She said that if she had known the answer, she would have told me.]</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Khi lời nói trực tiếp là câu điều kiện loại 3.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>✓</td><td colspan="2">John said, “If I had arrived here yesterday, I would have met her.” → John told me (that) if he had arrived there the day before, he would have met her.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>* Lưu ý: Trạng từ chỉ thời gian/nơi chốn vẫn thay đổi.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>✓</td><td colspan="2">“If it rains tomorrow, I will stay at home,” she said. → She said that if it rained the next day, she would stay at home.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Khi lời nói trực tiếp là câu điều kiện loại 1.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>✓</td><td colspan="2">The professor said, “If ice melts, it turns into water.” → The professor said if ice melts, it turns into water.</td></tr><tr><td rowspan="2">Khi lời nói trực tiếp là mệnh đề giả định theo sau wish, would rather, would sooner, it's time.</td><td rowspan="2">✓</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Victor said, "I wish I were there now." → Victor said that he wished he were there then.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Rooney said, "I would rather stay home tonight than go to the crowded places." → Rooney told me (that) she would rather stay home that night than go to the crowded places.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>* Lưu ý: Trạng từ chỉ thời gian/ nơi chốn vẫn thay đổi.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>✓</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>The tour guides said, "You needn't bring any food tomorrow." → The tour guides said to us (that) we needn't/ didn't have to bring any food the next day.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Khi lời nói trực tiếp các động từ tình thái (modal verbs) như would, could, should, might, ought to, had better, used to, need, must.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>✓</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Diana said, "You must take this chance." Diana told me (that) I must/ had to take that chance.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>- must có thể được giữ nguyên, hoặc đổi thành had to đều được. (giữ nguyên, nước, dùng nếu must được dùng để suy luận)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>✓</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>He said, "It must be very late now." → He said (that) it must be very late then.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>- needn't có thể giữ nguyên, hoặc đổi thành didn't have to.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>✓</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>The teacher said, "World War II ended in 1945." The teacher told us (that) World War II ended/had ended in 1945.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>* Lưu ý: Trạng từ chỉ thời gian/ nơi chốn vẫn thay đổi.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>✓</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>The teacher said, "World War II ended in 1945." The teacher told us (that) World War II ended/had ended in 1945.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Khi lời nói trực tiếp đề cập một sự kiện  lịch sử đã xác định rõ ràng về thời gian.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>✓</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>The teacher said, "World War II ended in 1945." The teacher told us (that) World War II ended/had ended in 1945.</td></tr></table>





Và một trường hợp đặc biệt khác, khi lời nói trực tiếp để cập một hành động đang diễn ra trong quá khứ (Past Continuous) thì có một hành động khác xen vào (Past Simple), ta có thể lùi MỘT trong hai thì đó.

- Với thì quá khứ tiếp diễn: giữ nguyên, hoặc đổi thành thì quá khứ hoàn thành tiếp diễn.

 Ex: My brother said, "I was studying when you called."  → My brother said (that) he was studying/ had been studying when I called.

- Với thì quá khứ đơn: giữ nguyên, hoặc đổi thành thì quá khứ hoàn thành.

 Ex: Helen said, "I was watching TV when the phone rang." Helen said (that) she was watching TV when the phone rang/ had rung.

## 2. Câu hỏi trong lời nói gián tiếp (Indirect Speech: Questions)

### a. Câu hỏi Yes-No (Yes-No Questions)

Khi chuyển đổi câu hỏi Yes-No từ lời nói trực tiếp sang lời nói gián tiếp, ta cần lưu ý những điểm sau:

- Sử dụng động từ tường thuật ask, inquire, wonder, want to know. Trong đó, ask có thể có tân ngữ theo sau (She asked me...), nhưng các từ còn lại thì không.

- Sử dụng if hoặc whether đế nối mệnh đề tường thuật với phần câu hỏi. Trong hầu hết các trường hợp, if và whether có thể thay thế cho nhau (whether thường sử dụng trong ngữ cảnh trang trọng hơn).

- Trong lời nói gián tiếp, đổi cấu trúc câu hỏi thành câu trần thuật (chủ ngữ dùng trước động từ).

- Tương tự như các loại câu tường thuật khác, thì của động từ trong câu hỏi Yes-No cũng cần được lùi lại, và các đại từ và trạng từ chỉ thời gian, nơi chốn cùng cần được thay đổi để phù hợp với ngữ cảnh tường thuật.

### S + asked/ wondered + if/ whether + S + V

 Ex: "Do you see this movie?" He asked Sam.

→ He asked Sam if/ whether he saw that movie.

"Will we visit Tom next week?" Lisa wondered.

→ Lisa wondered if/ whether they would visit the next week.

Trong trường hợp là câu hỏi đuôi (tag questions), ta vẫn theo các bước chuyển đổi như câu hỏi Yes-No, nhưng phần “hỏi đuôi” (don’t you, hasn’t she, won’t they...) sẽ bị loại bỏ.

Ex: "You like coffee, don't you?" She asked.

→ She asked me if/ whether I liked coffee.

"She has finished her homework, hasn't she?" Peter wondered.

→ Peter wanted to know if/ whether she had finished her homework.

### b. Câu hỏi Wh- (Wh-Questions)

Khi chuyển đổi câu hỏi Wh- từ lời nói trực tiếp sang lời nói gián tiếp, ta cần lưu ý những điểm sau:

- Sử dụng động từ tường thuật ask, inquire, wonder, want to know.

- Giữ nguyên từ để hỏi Wh- (what, when, where, why, who, whom, which, how)

- Bỏ dấu hỏi và thay đổi trật tự từ về dạng câu trần thuật.

- Lùi thi theo quy tắc lùi thì thông thường.

- Thay đổi đại từ, tính từ sở hữu và trạng từ chỉ thời gian, nơi chốn phù hợp trong câu gián tiếp.

S + asked + wondered + wh-word + S + V...

 Ex: "When will you arrive?" Selena asked.

→ Selena wanted to know when I would arrive.

"Why were you late yesterday?" Mr. Wilson asked.

→ Mr. Wilson asked her why she was late the previous day.

#### LƯU Ý:

Khi câu hỏi có cấu trúc wh-word + be + complement (bổ ngữ), động từ be có thể đặt trước hoặc sau bổ ngữ.

Ex: "Who's your father?" → He asked me who was my father/ who my father was.

"What's a math equation?" → She asked her father what was a math equation/ what a math equation was.

## 3. Câu mệnh lệnh/ yêu cầu trong lời nói gián tiếp (Indirect Speech: Command/Request)

- Các động từ tường thuật thường được sử dụng là: tell (yêu cầu), ask/request (yêu cầu), order/command (ra lệnh), beg (năn nỉ), ... Thông thường, các động từ này ở thì quá khứ đơn.

- Đặt tân ngữ chỉ người nhận yêu cầu ngay sau động từ tường thuật.

- Chuyển đổi động từ ở câu trực tiếp thành dạng to-infinitive.

- Thay đổi đại từ, tính từ sở hữu và trạng từ chỉ thời gian, nơi chốn phù hợp trong câu gián tiếp.

S + asked/told + O (+ not) + to-infinitive

 Ex: "Close the door!" the teacher said to the students.→ The teacher told the students to close the door.

“Don’t make noise in the library, please!” the librarian said. → The librarian told us not to make noise in the library.

Ngoài ra, ta còn có thể sử dụng cấu trúc ask/beg for + noun phrase

Ex: "Could you give me the menu, please?" → She asked for the menu.

“Please, just a little money!” → He begged for a little money.

4. Câu đề nghị/ lời hứa/ lời khuyên, lời mời trong lời nói gián tiếp (Indirect Speech: Offer/ Promises/ Advice/ Invitation)

Ta thường sử dụng các động từ sau: offer, recommend, promise, advise, encourage, invite, agree, remind, warn, urge ...

S + offered/ advised (+ O) + to-infinitive

 Ex: Jim said, "You should exercise regularly."  → Jim advised me to exercise regularly. She said, "I will show you around the city."  → She offered to show me around the city.

### BẢNG CÁC ĐỘNG TỪ TƯỜNG THUẬT

<div style="text-align: center;"><div style="text-align: center;">• V + O + to-infinitive</div> </div>




<table border="1" style="margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;"><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Động từ</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Ý nghĩa</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Ví dụ</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">tell</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">yêu cầu/ bảo</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">"Clean your room!" → My mother told me to clean my room.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">ask</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">yêu câu</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">"Waiter! Bring me the bill, please!" → He asked the waiter to bring him the bill.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">request</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">yêu câu</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">"Bring me the check." → The customer requested the waiter to bring the check.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">advise</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">khuyên</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">"Dad! Don't smoke here." I advised my father not to smoke here.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">allow</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">cho phép</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">"Don't ever skip breakfast!" → Jessica never allows her daughter to skip breakfast.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">permit</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">cho phép</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">"OK, you can take pictures." → The tour guide permits tourists to take pictures.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">beg</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">cầu xin</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">"Please, don't tell my dad about this." → He begged me not to tell his dad about that.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">encourage</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">khuyến khích</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">"Come on! Just apply for promotion." → Larry encouraged his colleague to apply for promotion.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">forbid</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">cấm</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">"You mustn't come near my house." → The man forbade the children to come near his house.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">order</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">ra lệnh</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">"Move forward!" → The captain ordered the crew to move forward.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">command</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">ra lệnh</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">"I want you to defend the castle." → The king commanded his knights to defend the castle.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">invite</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">mời</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">"Would you like to join the local competition?" → They invited me to join the local competition.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">recommend</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">khuyên/ đề nghị</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">"You should get more rest." → The doctor recommended the patient to get more rest.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">remind</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">nhắc nhở</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">"Remember to review for your test." → I reminded my younger brother to review for his test.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">urge</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">thúc giục/ năn nỉ</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">"Listen! You need to take action now." → We urged the manager to take action then.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">warn</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">cảnh báo</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">"You'd better stay close to the shore while swimming." → The lifeguard warned the children to stay close to the shore while swimming.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">instruct</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">hướng dẫn</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">"I'll show you how to restart the computer."→Kelly is instructing his son to restart the computer."We will be happy if you become a doctor." → My parents expected me to become a doctor.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">expect</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">kì vọng</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">"We will be happy if you become a doctor." → My parents expected me to become a doctor.</td></tr></table>




##### • V + to-infinitive



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Động từ</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Ý nghĩa</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Ví dụ</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>offer</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>đề nghị</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>&quot;Shall I drive you to the airport?&quot; → He offered to drive us to the airport.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>demand</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>đòi hỏi</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>&quot;I want to speak to the manager.&quot; → The woman demanded to speak to the manager.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>agree</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>đồng ý</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>&quot;OK, I&#x27;ll sign the contract.&quot; → Mr. Brown finally agreed to sign the contract.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>promise</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>hứa</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>&quot;I&#x27;ll visit my grandparents more often.&quot; → She promised to visit her grandparents more often.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>refuse</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>từ chối</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>&quot;I&#x27;m sorry, but I won&#x27;t attend the wedding celebration.&quot; → Maltida refused to attend the wedding celebration.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>guarantee</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>dám bảo</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>&quot;Sure! We will deliver the product within three business days.&quot; → The company guarantees to deliver the product within three business days.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>threaten</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>đe doạ</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>&quot;I&#x27;ll cancel your project if your team didn&#x27;t meet the deadline.&quot; → He threatened to cancel the project if her team didn&#x27;t meet the deadline.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>want</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>mong muốn</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>&quot;I wish I lived in a bigger house.&quot; → She wanted/wished to live in a bigger house.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>= wish</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>swear</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>thề</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>&quot;I would tell the truth in court. I swear!&quot; → The man swore to tell the truth in court.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>volunteer</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>tình nguyện</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>&quot;Let me tutor younger children in math.&quot; → The students volunteered to tutor younger children in math.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>expect</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>mong dọi</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>&quot;We are hopeful that we will see a large crowd at the concert.&quot; → The girl band expected to see a large crowd at the concert.</td></tr></table>

### • V+Gerund (V-ing)


<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Động từ</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Ý nghĩa</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Ví dụ</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>admit</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>thừa nhận</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>&quot;Yes, I broke the vase.&quot; → The child admitted having broken the vase.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>deny</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>chối bỏ</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>&quot;No, I don&#x27;t cheat on the test.&quot; → She denied cheating on the test.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>recommend</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>khuyên/ đề nghị</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>&quot;Shall we try the seafood pasta at this restaurant?&quot; → Anna recommends trying the seafood pasta at that restaurant.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>suggest</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>đề nghị/ gợi ý</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>&quot;Why don&#x27;t we watch this new action movie?&quot; → He suggested watching that new action movie.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>advise</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>khuyên</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>&quot;You should check the weather forecast before going hiking.&quot; → They advise checking the weather forecast before going hiking.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>propose</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>đề nghị/ đề xuất</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>&quot;How about organising a charity event to raise funds&quot; → They proposed organising a charity event to raise funds.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>imagine</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>tưởng tượng</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>&quot;Every time I close my eyes, I think I&#x27;m living on another planet.&quot; → My little sister often imagined living on another planet.</td></tr></table>

### • V+prep.+Gerund/Noun



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Động từ</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Ý nghĩa</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Ví dụ</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>apologise (to sb) for sth</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>xin lỗi</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>"I'm sorry to paint in your notebook." → Bob apologised to his sister for painting in her notebook.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>insist on</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>đòi/nài nỉ làm gì</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>"I have to meet Mr. Brown immediately." → The customer insists on meeting Mr. Brown immediately.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>complain about</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>phàn nàn</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>"They're always playing their music loudly." → We complained about their playing music loudly.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>accuse sb of</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>buộc tội</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>"You stole my watch! It's you." → The neighbour accused the little boy of stealing his watch.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>object to</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>phản đối</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>"No, you're too young to travel alone." → Her parents object to her traveling alone at such a young age.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>confess to</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>thừa nhận</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>"Yes, I broke the windows."→ Nick confessed to breaking the windows.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>criticise sb for</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>chỉ trích</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>"I can't believe it. Why did you cheat?"→ The teacher criticised the student for cheating.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>congratulate sb on</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>chúc mừng</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>"Congratulation! You've got the promotion."→ Ray congratulate me on getting the promotion.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>inform sb about</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>thông báo</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>"I have something to tell you. Your son failed the exam."→ The teacher informed the parents about their son failing the exam.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>prohibit sb from</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>ngăn cản/ cấm</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>"You mustn't camp here."→ The park owner prohibits people from camping there.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>scold sb for</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>trách/ la mắng</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>"Where were you? I remember you're allowed to stay out too late."→ My parents scolded me for staying out too late.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>thank sb for</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>cảm ơn</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>"I really appreciate your help."→ We thanked them for helping us.</td></tr></table>



##### • V + that + subjunctive

Câu giả định hay thế giả định (subjunctive) là một dạng ngữ pháp dùng để diễn tả những điều không có thật, điều ước, tình huống giả định, khả năng, nguyện vọng, đê xuất, yêu cầu, mệnh lệnh.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-15//40023c9c-a29b-45e3-8060-7acb057be440/markdown_1/imgs/img_in_image_box_317_876_862_914.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-05-07T14%3A41%3A53Z%2F-1%2F%2F4d6eb88a037f39efbb157598356f9ec5a23a6f6fd150fd2dbd932cbe50ad7427" alt="Image" width="45%" /></div>


<div style="text-align: center;"><div style="text-align: center;">Một số động từ giả định thường được sử dụng trước mệnh đề that:</div> </div>




<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>advise</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>demand</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>prefer</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>require</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>ask</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>insist</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>propose</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>stipulate</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>command</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>move</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>recommend</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>suggest</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>decree</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>order</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>request</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>urge</td></tr></table>

Ex: The doctor urged that the patient stop smoking.

The consultant advised that the company invest in new technology.

The boss requested that all the doors be locked at night.

#### LƯU Ý:

Một số động từ như suggest, recommend, ask, insist,... ta có thể thêm should trong mệnh đề that để thuật lại lời khuyên, lời yêu cầu, lời gợi ý.

Ex: She insisted that he (should) apologise for his behavior.

I recommend that you (should) see a lawyer.5. Câu cảm thán và câu trả lời yes/no trong lời nói gián tiếp (Indirect Speech: Exclamation/ Yes-No answers)

## a. Câu cảm thán trong lời nói gián tiếp

Khi chuyến câu cảm thán từ lời nói trực tiếp sang lời nói gián tiếp, chúng ta thường sử dụng các động từ tường thuật như exclaim (thốt lên), shout/scream (la lớn), cry out (la lên), say (nói), declare (tuyên bố), comment (nhận xét), utter (thốt ra) kết hợp với that hoặc dùng cấu trúc exclaim with delight/surprise/horror/joy.

• S + exclaimed/ shouted + that + S + V

 Ex: "What a beautiful song!" she exclaimed.→ She exclaimed that it was a beautiful song.

"How badly he behaved!" Donna exclaimed. → Donna exclaimed that he had behaved badly.

"She was so kind a woman!" they commented. → They commented that she was such a kind woman.

• S + exclaimed/ shouted + with delight/ surprise/ horror/ joy + that + S + V

 Ex: "How wonderful the view is!" he shouted.→ He shouted with delight that the view was wonderful.

“What a serious accident!” Matt said. → Matt exclaimed with horror that the accident was serious.

#### Một số trường hợp khác

– “Fantastic!” said Lola. → Lola said (with delight) that it was fantastic.

– “Such an adorable cat!” Melanie commented. → Melanie commented that the cat was adorable.

– “Good!” she exclaimed. → She exclaimed with pleasure.

– “Happy birthday!” said John. → John wished me a happy birthday.

– “Thank you!” my brother said. → My brother thanked me.

## b. Câu trả lời Yes/ No

Để tường thuật câu trả lời yes hoặc no, chúng ta dùng chủ ngữ và trợ động từ tương

ứng trong câu gián tiếp. (subject + auxiliary verb).

Ex: Demi asked, "Do you like basketball?" and I said "Yes". → Demi asked me if/whether I liked basketball and I said I did.

Her father asked, "Have you done your test well at school?" and she said "No". → Her father asked if/ whether she had done her test well at school and she said she hadn't.

## IV. Các loại câu hỗn hợp trong lời nói gián tiếp (Mixed types in indirect speech)

Lời nói gián tiếp đôi khi bao gồm nhiều loại câu khác nhau trong cùng một phát ngôn, tạo thành câu hỗn hợp. Dưới đây là các loại câu hỗn hợp phổ biến trong lời nói gián tiếp và cách chuyển đổi chúng:

<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Loại câu</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Ví dụ</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Câu trần thuật kết hợp câu hỏi</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>&quot;I&#x27;m very tired today. Can you help me with this work?&quot; she said. → She said (that) she was very tired that day and asked if I could help her with that work.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Câu trần thuật kết hợp câu mệnh lệnh</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>&quot;I don&#x27;t have time now. Please come back tomorrow,&quot; the manager said. → The manager said he didn&#x27;t have time then and asked us to come back the next day.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Câu hỏi kết hợp với câu mệnh lệnh</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>&quot;Where have you been? Don&#x27;t ever disappear like that again,&quot; his mother said. → His mother asked where he had been and told him not to ever disappear like that again.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Câu cảm thán kết hợp với câu trần thuật</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>&quot;What a beautiful day! I think we should go for a picnic,&quot; John said. → John exclaimed that it was a beautiful day and suggested that they should go for a picnic.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Câu hỏi kết hợp với câu cảm thán</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>&quot;Why are you so late? How worried I&#x27;ve been!&quot; his wife said. → His wife asked why he was so late and exclaimed that she had been very worried.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Câu trần thuật kết hợp với câu Yes/No</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>&quot;I need your help with this project. Will you join my team?&quot; she asked and he replied &quot;Yes, I&#x27;d be happy to.&quot; → She said she needed his help with that project and asked him if whether he would join her team. He replied that he would be happy to.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Câu mệnh lệnh kết hợp với câu điều kiện</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>&quot;Take your umbrella. If it rains, you&#x27;ll get wet,&quot; my mother warned. → My mother told me to take my umbrella and warned that if it rained, I would get wet.</td></tr></table>

Tóm lại, khi chuyển đổi các câu hỗn hợp trong lời nói gián tiếp, cần lưu ý:

Sử dụng từ nối thích hợp (and, but, that, if, whether, etc.) để liên kết các phần của câu.

Thay đổi động từ tường thuật cho phù hợp với từng loại câu (said, asked, told, exclaimed, ordered, etc.)

- Áp dụng các quy tắc chuyển đổi thì, đại từ, và các từ chỉ thời gian, nơi chốn cho từng phần của câu.

Đảm bảo tính mạch lạc và thống nhất trong toàn bộ câu hỗn hợp.



---

# CHƯƠNG 5 — SỰ PHỐI HỢP THÌ (Sequence of Tenses)


<!-- ===== SEQUENCE OF TENSES ===== -->

# SỰ PHỐI HỢP THÌ TRONG CÁC CẤU TRÚC CÂU KHÁC (SEQUENCE OF TENSES)

### Kiến thức cần nhớ:

- Định nghĩa và công thức của các thì đã học ở Chương 2.

- Các quy tắc phối hợp thì thường gặp.

## Mục tiêu học tập:

- Nắm rõ các thì nào thường hay xuất hiện chung trong một câu.

- Biết cách sử dụng các liên từ chỉ thời gian khi phối hợp thì.

- Biết cách chia thì trong mệnh đề If, Wish/ If only, và câu tường thuật (Reported Speech).

Phối hợp thi là nguyên tắc yêu cầu sự nhất quán giữa các thì trong câu có nhiều mệnh đề. Một câu có thể bao gồm một mệnh đề chính (main clause) và một hoặc nhiều mệnh đề phụ (subordinate clause). Khi một câu có hai hoặc nhiều mệnh đề, động từ trong các mệnh đề phải phù hợp với nhau về thì.

## I. Mệnh đề chính và Mệnh đề phụ (Main clause and Subordinate clause)

Mệnh đề chính (Main Clause) là một mệnh đề độc lập, có thể dùng một mình và diễn đạt một ý hoàn chỉnh.

Mệnh đề phụ (Subordinate Clause) là một mệnh đề không thể đứng một mình mà phải đi kèm với mệnh đề chính để tạo thành một câu có nghĩa.

☑ She was happy because she passed the exam.

Mệnh đề chính: She was happy.

Mệnh đề phụ: because she passed the exam.

♦ I will call you later when I get home.

Mệnh đề chính: I will call you later.

Mệnh đề phụ: when I get home.

## II. Các quy tắc phối hợp thì quan trọng (Important Rules of Sequence of Tenses)

a. Khi mệnh đề chính ở các thì HIỆN TẠI hoặc TƯƠNG LAI → Mệnh đề phụ có thể ở các thì sau:



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>MỆNH ĐỀ CHÍNH</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>MỆNH ĐỀ PHỤ</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>VÍ DỤ</td></tr><tr><td rowspan="5">Present Simple</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Present Simple</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>He drinks coffee whenever he feels tired.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Present Continuous</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>She tells me that everyone is preparing for the presentation.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Present Perfect</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Ralph feels very happy because he has achieved his goals.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Future Simple</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>They study English diligently since they will move to Canada soon.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>“Be going to”</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>We clean the house thoroughly because guests are going to stay overnight.</td></tr><tr><td rowspan="5">Present Continuous</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Present Simple (thường nói về sự thật, chân lý hiển nhiên, hoặc thói quen)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Bobby is explaining that he always checks his email every morning.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Present Continuous</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Miley is cleaning the house while her sister is cooking dinner.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Present Perfect</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>I am reading the book that you have recommended.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Future Simple</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>My family is saving money because we will buy a house next year.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>“Be going to”</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Now we are preparing extra food for the cats since we are going to be away this weekend.</td></tr><tr><td rowspan="4">Present Perfect / Present Perfect Continuous</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Present Simple</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>I have forgotten what he looks like.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Present Continuous</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>We haven't finished the green project yet because Gary is still working on it.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Past Simple (có thời điểm cụ thể)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>She has lived in Paris since she was a child.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Present Perfect • ít thông dụng, thường dùng để diễn tả sự kiện vẫn chưa kết thúc ở mệnh đề trạng ngữ chỉ thời gian.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>I haven't been able to concentrate since I have had this headache. (cơn đau đầu vẫn còn tiếp diễn)</td></tr><tr><td rowspan="3">Future Simple</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Present Simple</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>She will phone me when she arrives.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Present Continuous</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>I will join if you are still playing basketball for that team.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Present Perfect</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Kevin will meet you after he has done all his tasks.</td></tr><tr><td rowspan="2">Be going to</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Present Simple</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>I am going to leave when the rain stops.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Present Perfect</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>She is going to call him immediately when she has received the package.</td></tr></table>





b. Khi mệnh đề chính ở các thì QUÁ KHỨ → Mệnh đề phụ có thể ở các thì sau:



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>MỆNH ĐỀ CHÍNH</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>MỆNH ĐỀ PHỤ</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>VÍ DỤ</td></tr><tr><td rowspan="3">Past Simple</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Past Simple</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>They visited the museum because they loved history.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Past Continuous</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>He discovered the solution while he was walking in the park.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Past Perfect</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>I came to see her, but she had already gone.</td></tr><tr><td rowspan="3">Past Continuous</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Past Simple</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>She was sleeping when the phone rang.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Past Continuous</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>While I was studying, my roommate was listening to music.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Past Perfect</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Laura was feeling tired because she hadn't slept well the night before.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Past Perfect / Past Perfect Continuous</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Past Simple</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>We had already eaten dinner before she arrived.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Would + V bare-in</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Past Simple</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>They would go to the beach if the weather was nicer.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Tương lai trong quá khứ</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Past Perfect</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>If he hadn't missed his flight last night, he would be here with us now.</td></tr></table>





Các cấu trúc khác dùng để phối hợp thì trong tiếng Anh

Câu gián tiếp (Reported Speech)

Khi tường thuật lại lời nói của người khác, nếu động từ tường thuật ở thì quá khứ, ta cần phải lùi thì (backshift).

Quy tác lùi thì:



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Câu trực tiếp (Direct Speech)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Câu gián tiếp (Reported Speech)</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Present Simple → Past Simple</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>“I like ice cream,” she said. → She said (that) she liked ice cream.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Present Continuous → Past Continuous</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>“Where are you going?” he asked. → He asked me where I was going.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Present Perfect → Past Perfect</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>“Have you finished your homework?” she asked them. → She asked if/ whether they had finished their homework.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Past Simple → Past Perfect</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>“I met him yesterday,” she said. → She told me (that) she had met him the previous day.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Future Simple (will) → Would</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>“I will call you,” he said. → He said (that) he would call me.</td></tr></table>

* xem chi tiết cách lùi thì ở phần Câu tường thuật (Reported Speech) trong Chương 4. Câu điều kiện (Conditional Sentences)

Các câu điều kiện cũng yêu cầu sự phối hợp giữa các thì trong mệnh đề If và mệnh đề chính.



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Loại</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Mệnh đề If</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Mệnh đề chính</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Ví dụ</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Loại 0 (Zero Conditional)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Present Simple</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Present Simple</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>If you heat water to 100°C, it boils.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Loại 1 (First Conditional)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Present Simple</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Future Simple (will)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>If it rains, we will stay at home.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Loại 2 (Second Conditional)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Past Simple</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Would +  V (bare-inf.)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>If I were rich, I would travel the world.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Loại 3 (Third Conditional)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Past Perfect</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Would have + past participle</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>If you had studied, you would have passed the exam.</td></tr></table>





xem chi tiết ở phần Câu điều kiện (Conditional Sentences) trong Chương 4.

##### Câu ước (Wish & If only Sentences)

Cấu trúc này dùng để diễn tả mong ước về một điều KHÔNG có thật ở hiện tại, quá khứ hoặc tương lai.



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Thời gian ước muốn</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Cấu trúc</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Ví dụ</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Hiện tại</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>S + wish(es) + Past Simple</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>I wish I was/ were taller.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Quá khứ</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>S + wish(es) + Past Perfect</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>She wishes she had studied harder.</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Tương lai</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>S + wish(es) + Would + V barc-inf.</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>I wish it would stop raining.</td></tr></table>

##### LƯU Ý:

Nếu chủ ngữ là I, he, she, it và danh từ số ít, ta có thể sử dụng cả was và were.

Ex. She wishes she was/ were a doctor.

If only it wasn't/ weren't raining.

- Để thể hiện cảm giác phiền phức về một điều gì đó, ta sử dụng wish + would(n't)

Ex: I wish the neighbour wouldn't play loud music.

He wishes his son wouldn't interrupt him when he is working.

* xem chi tiết ở phần Mệnh đề sau Wish và If Only (Clauses after Wish and If Only) trong Chương 3.

- Câu so sánh thời gian khi sử dụng với Before, After, When, By the time, As soon as Những cấu trúc này cũng đòi hỏi sự phối hợp thì đúng giữa hai mệnh đề. Lưu ý, có thể đặt các liên từ chỉ thời gian ở đầu câu hoặc giữa câu, và liên từ luôn ĐI VỚI mệnh đề phụ.



<table border="1" style="margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;"><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Liên từ</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Mệnh đề phụ</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Mệnh đề chính</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Ví dụ</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Before</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Past Simple</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Past Perfect</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Before she went out, she had turned off the lights.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">After</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Past Perfect</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Past Simple</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">After Brian had left, we closed the door.</td></tr><tr><td rowspan="2">When</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Past Simple</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Past Continuous</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">I was watching TV when he called.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Past Continuous</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Past Simple</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">When she was walking to the store, she met an old friend.</td></tr><tr><td rowspan="2">While</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Past Continuous</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Past Simple</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">While I was reading a book, the light went out.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Past Continuous</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Past Continuous</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">We were painting the fence while Tom was cooking lunch.</td></tr><tr><td rowspan="2">By the time</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Past Simple</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Past Perfect</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">By the time I arrived, they had left.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Present Simple</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Future Perfect Tense</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">By the time he graduates, he will have completed all his coursework.</td></tr><tr><td rowspan="3">As soon as</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Present Simple</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Future Simple</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">As soon as he arrives, we will start the meeting.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Past Simple</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Past Simple</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">He started eating as soon as the food was placed on the table.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Past Perfect</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Past Simple</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">He called his parents as soon as he had arrived at the hotel.</td></tr><tr><td rowspan="4">Until</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Present Simple</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Future Simple</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">I will wait here until you come back.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Present Simple</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Future Perfect</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">We will have done the cleaning until the guests arrive.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Past Simple</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Past Simple</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Josh waited patiently until the bus came.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Past Simple</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">Past Perfect</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">They had lived in that house for ten years until they moved to Australia.</td></tr></table>
